备注：The page-3 arrow says AcOH (1.0 equiv) under standard conditions, whose Scheme-2 definition uses Pd(OAc)2 (10 mol%); page 8 instead reports Pd(OAc)2 4.4 mg, 0.02 mmol with 1a-d6 0.3 mmol. Both are retained as a source conflict.

备注：The page-8 source explicitly reports DMBQ as 30.0 mg, 0.22 mmol, 1.1 equiv and 1a-d6 as 0.3 mmol; the numbers are internally inconsistent and were not corrected.

备注：The phrase 'allowed to warm to room temperature' after a 90 °C hold is preserved verbatim and not changed to 'cool'.

待核查：Pd(OAc)2 loading conflicts between page-3 standard conditions (10 mol%) and page-8 quantities (0.02 mmol Pd vs 0.3 mmol 1a-d6); no value was chosen.；Page 8 reports DMBQ as both 0.22 mmol and 1.1 equivalents against 0.3 mmol 1a-d6; these values are internally inconsistent.；The amount of diazo ester 2a is not reported in the page-8 procedure.；The final chromatography EtOAc/petroleum ether ratio is not reported.；The analytical method used to establish absence of deuterium scrambling is not reported.
